## Session decisions (2026-07-03) — Jarvis OS / Fable prompt prep

### Vault state at session start
- dashboard: dead Dataview queries (4), skills not muscle memory, no tracker data
- convergence Moves 1-4 (North Star) partially done — none fully complete
- jarvis-memory in .mcp.json, never verified to start
- 50+ items in 05_Clippings never distilled
- 20_Progress/AI/ has full dump of: Claude Code (CausalOps/Jarvis/Portfolio/Resq/OpsPilot/TradingView + Windows Home), Cursor, Kiro, Codex setups

**Why:** Preparing a comprehensive Fable 5 prompt to "almost finish" Jarvis OS

**How to apply:** When the Fable prompt is written, reference these decisions as ground truth

### Dashboard 1: 00_Dashboard.md (Operations Center)
- Layout: mimic screenshot (two-column, card tiles, progress bars, Today's Focus header)
- Theme: AnuPpuccin (dark) + new `dashboard.css` snippet for card layout
- Meta Bind: installed, use for interactive inputs and progress bars
- Obsidian Bases: built-in, use for database views
- Auto-open: Homepage plugin (install) OR Workspaces Plus (already installed)
- /startday: updates a `## Today's Focus` section at top of dashboard (goal + 80/20 + deadlines)
- /closeday: asks user questions (lc_count, study_hours, habits_done, wins) → writes to daily note frontmatter → dashboard queries via Dataview
- Manual entry: Meta Bind INPUT fields in dashboard for things not auto-computed

**Metrics to show:**
- LeetCode: daily count (Meta Bind input) + weekly total (Dataview sum of lc_count from daily notes)
- Study hours: daily (Meta Bind input) + weekly total
- Habit streak: Dataview count of consecutive days with habits_done non-empty
- Project commits: Meta Bind manual input (can't auto-query GitHub)
- Internship applications sent: Dataview count from Career/Internships/Tracker.md
- Coding tasks done: Dataview from Tasks plugin
- 5-wins completion rate: Dataview from daily note wins field
- Reviews done weekly: Dataview count of weekly/daily review files
- Clippings ingested: Dataview count of 60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries/ files created this week

**Data architecture:** Each daily note frontmatter gets: `lc_count`, `study_hours`, `habits_done`, `wins`
/closeday writes these. Dashboard queries last 7 daily notes.

### Dashboard 2: Claude OS (Agentic OS visualization)
- Two parts: Dataview registry note (queryable) + Excalidraw canvas (visual, clickable)
- Registry: `60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Claude OS.md` — Dataview table of all skills/agents/hooks/MCPs
- Excalidraw: `10_Areas/Excalidraw/Claude OS Map.excalidraw` — branches per tool (Claude Code / Cursor / Kiro / Codex)
- Source material: `20_Progress/AI/` folder (all project .claude setups already in vault)
- Inventory found:
  - Jarvis: 5 agents, 13 commands, 15 skills
  - CausalOps: 3 agents, 4 commands
  - Windows Home: 31 firecrawl skills
  - everything-claude-code marketplace: 40+ skills

### Plugins
- Install before Fable: `obsidian-homepage` (auto-open dashboard on startup)
- Already installed: Meta Bind ✓, Dataview ✓, Bases (built-in) ✓, Tasks ✓, Style Settings ✓, Workspaces Plus ✓
- Theme: AnuPpuccin (dark) — write `dashboard.css` for card layout

### Scope for Fable
- Wire and verify jarvis-memory MCP (already in .mcp.json, needs pip install mcp + smoke test)
- Build 00_Dashboard.md (new version) + dashboard.css snippet
- Update /startday to patch Today's Focus in dashboard
- Update /closeday to ask questions + write frontmatter
- Create Claude OS registry note + Excalidraw canvas
- Ingest 60_Claude/05_Clippings/ (50+ PDFs + web clips — meaty ones in detail, lightweight as quick summaries)
- Pull second-brain-claudekit + CausalOps .claude into vault as notes (light-touch catalog per repo)
- Add frontmatter to all notes missing it (exceptions: log.md, AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, HUMAN_WRITING.md, index/MOC files, README files)
- Repair 10_Areas: Life/ needs tracker notes (Health, Habits, Finances, Career, Learning, Relationships)
- Definitive domain files in 10_Areas (source-of-truth, can be long)
- Cross-link UMN notes → Career/ and Trading/
- SKIP: TradingView folder (read-only, no edits)

### 10_Areas architecture decision
- Each domain folder = 1-2 SOURCE OF TRUTH files (definitive, can be very long)
- All details/projects defined elsewhere and linked back
- Life/ = full life OS: Health, Habits, Finances, Career trajectory, Learning, Relationships/Mentors

### Key constraints for Fable
- TradingView folder: can READ, cannot EDIT
- HUMAN_WRITING.md governs voice — no AI slop
- Prefer patching existing notes over creating new ones
- Search before creating
- Log session at end

/* ============================================================
   Jarvis Dashboard — card layout, stat tiles, grid
   Works with AnuPpuccin (dark) + Meta Bind + Dataview
   ============================================================ */

/* ── Two-column page layout ── */
.dashboard-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  gap: 16px;
  margin-top: 12px;
}

/* ── Section card ── */
.card {
  background: var(--background-secondary);
  border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 14px 16px;
  margin-bottom: 12px;
}

.card-accent {
  border-left: 3px solid var(--color-green);
}

.card-warning {
  border-left: 3px solid var(--color-yellow);
}

/* ── Section label (small caps above heading) ── */
.section-label {
  font-size: 0.65rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--color-green);
  margin-bottom: 4px;
}

/* ── Current focus headline ── */
.focus-headline {
  font-size: 1.5rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--color-green);
  line-height: 1.25;
  margin: 4px 0 8px 0;
}

/* ── Stat tile grid ── */
.stat-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(120px, 1fr));
  gap: 10px;
  margin: 10px 0;
}

.stat-tile {
  background: var(--background-primary-alt);
  border: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 0.65rem;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  margin-bottom: 4px;
}

.stat-value {
  font-size: 1.6rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--text-normal);
  line-height: 1;
}

.stat-delta {
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  margin-top: 3px;
}

.stat-delta.positive { color: var(--color-green); }
.stat-delta.negative { color: var(--color-red); }
.stat-delta.neutral  { color: var(--text-muted); }

/* ── Progress bar (complements Meta Bind's built-in) ── */
.progress-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  margin: 6px 0;
  font-size: 0.85rem;
}

.progress-bar-track {
  flex: 1;
  height: 6px;
  background: var(--background-modifier-border);
  border-radius: 3px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.progress-bar-fill {
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--color-green);
  border-radius: 3px;
  transition: width 0.3s ease;
}

/* ── Daily driver checkbox rows ── */
.driver-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  padding: 5px 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  font-size: 0.875rem;
}

.driver-row:last-child { border-bottom: none; }

.driver-row.done {
  text-decoration: line-through;
  color: var(--text-muted);
}

/* ── Callout overrides for dashboard use ── */
.callout[data-callout="focus"] {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-green) 8%, var(--background-secondary));
  border-color: var(--color-green);
}

.callout[data-callout="focus"] .callout-title {
  color: var(--color-green);
}

/* ── Navigation pill row ── */
.nav-pills {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
  margin: 8px 0;
}

.nav-pill {
  background: var(--background-modifier-border);
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 2px 10px;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  text-decoration: none;
}

.nav-pill:hover {
  background: var(--color-green);
  color: var(--text-on-accent);
}

/* ── Reading-mode only: hide edit hints ── */
.is-live-preview .mb-hide-in-preview { display: none; }

```

## Metrics

### This Week

```dataviewjs
const folder = '"10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"';
const pages = dv.pages(folder).where(p => {
  if (!p.file.day) return false;
  const today = dv.date("today");
  const monday = today.minus({days: today.weekday - 1});
  return p.file.day >= monday && p.file.day <= today;
});

const lcTotal = pages.map(p => p.lc_count || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const studyTotal = pages.map(p => p.study_today || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const winsTotal = pages.map(p => p.wins_done || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const daysLogged = pages.length;

dv.paragraph(`**LeetCode:** ${lcTotal} / 35 target · **Study:** ${studyTotal}h · **Wins:** ${winsTotal} / ${daysLogged * 5} · **Days logged:** ${daysLogged}`);
```

| Metric | Today | Weekly target |
|--------|-------|---------------|
| LeetCode problems | `VIEW[{lc_today}]` | 35 |
| Study hours | `VIEW[{study_today}]` h | 25 h |
| 5-wins completed | `VIEW[{wins_done}]` / 5 | 35 |

name: closing-day
description: Scores the day, asks structured questions to capture metrics, writes frontmatter data to today's note, and updates the dashboard's Today section.
---
# closeday

**Usage:** `/closeday`

---

## When to Invoke

At the end of the work day. Works on the daily note `/startday` already filled. No separate file created.

---

## Instructions

### Step 0 — Find Today's Note

Locate `10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily/YYYY-MM-DD.md` for today.

- Exists with Morning Plan → proceed to Step 1.
- Exists but no Morning Plan → tell user, proceed anyway with what's available.
- Doesn't exist → create it with frontmatter from `30_Order/Templates/Enumerate/Better Today.md`, proceed.

---

### Step 1 — Auto-gather What Happened

Before asking the user anything, read:

1. `60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Session Logs/log.md` — all entries with today's date
2. `10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily/YYYY-MM-DD.md` — Morning Plan checkboxes
3. Files modified in `20_Progress/` today (by mtime) — proxy for project commits
4. Files created in `60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries/` today — clippings ingested count

Build a draft scorecard from this before talking to the user.

---

### Step 2 — Ask the User (structured, one block)

Ask all questions at once. Do not ask them one-by-one.

```
Closing the day — quick check:

1. LeetCode today: how many problems? (target ≥5)
2. Study hours today: total focused study time? (e.g., 2.5)
3. Which 5-wins did you hit? (Physical / Project / Career / Cleanup / Review — list them)
4. Habits done today? (e.g., gym, lc-5, csci2033, course-step, review)
5. Anything to add, blocked, or carried forward?
```

Accept a free-text reply. Parse it for the five data points.

---

### Step 3 — Update Today's Note Frontmatter

Patch the frontmatter of `10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily/YYYY-MM-DD.md` with the values from Step 2:

```yaml
lc_count: <number from answer 1>
study_today: <hours from answer 2>
wins_done: <count of wins listed in answer 3>
habits_done: [<list from answer 4>]
```

This is what the dashboard Dataview queries read. Get these right.

---

### Step 4 — Patch the Dashboard Today's Focus

Clear the "today_focus", "today_80", "today_20" fields in `00_Dashboard.md` frontmatter so `/startday` starts fresh tomorrow:

```yaml
today_focus: ""
today_80: ""
today_20: ""
lc_today: 0
study_today: 0
wins_done: 0
```

---

### Step 5 — Score and Append to Daily Note

Append below the Morning Plan section in today's note:

```markdown
## End of Day

### Scorecard

| Track     | Target   | Result | Met? |
|-----------|----------|--------|------|
| LeetCode  | ≥5       | N      | ✓/✗  |
| CSCI 4041 | 25-45 min|        | ✓/✗  |
| CSCI 2033 | 30-45 min|        | ✓/✗  |
| MATH 2230 | board    |        | ✓/✗  |
| HIST 1103 | step/N/A |        | ✓/✗  |
| 5 Wins    | 5/5      | N/5    | ✓/✗  |

**Day: GREEN** ← if LC ≥5 AND ≥4/5 other rows met. Otherwise **RED**.

### What Happened
[2-3 honest sentences. No inflation. What got done, what didn't.]

### Friction Fix
*(One behavioral change for tomorrow — only if RED)*

### Tomorrow's First Move
[One concrete task to start with tomorrow]
```

Fill checkboxes from auto-gathered data (Step 1) and user input (Step 2). If unverifiable, mark `(?)` not ✓.

---

### Step 6 — Append Session Log

Append to `60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Session Logs/log.md`:

```markdown
## [YYYY-MM-DD] closeday | Daily close

- Note: [[10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily/YYYY-MM-DD]]
- Status: GREEN / RED
- LeetCode: N problems
- Study: Nh
- Wins: N/5 (list them)
- Carryover: [open items rolling tomorrow]
- Tomorrow: [first move]
```

---

## Constraints

- Never create `60_Claude/30_Reviews/Closeday - YYYY-MM-DD.md` — daily note is the record.
- Never modify the Morning Plan section — only append below.
- If scorecard already exists, ask before overwriting.
- Be honest about unverified rows — mark `(?)` not ✓.
- The Step 3 frontmatter patch is the most important output — the dashboard reads from it.

```
- `Read` -- `D:\Users\_Anant\10_Areas\Documents\Jarvis\.claude\skills\startday.md`
- `Edit` -- `D:\Users\_Anant\10_Areas\Documents\Jarvis\.claude\skills\startday.md`
```diff

+ ### Step 3b — Patch the Dashboard Today's Focus

After patching the daily note, also patch `00_Dashboard.md` frontmatter with today's focus data so the dashboard header updates immediately:

```yaml
today_focus: "[one-line headline objective for today]"
today_80: "[the one task that makes today a success]"
today_20: "[2-3 supporting tasks as a comma-separated string]"
```

Use `vault_patch` to update only these three frontmatter fields. Do not touch any other frontmatter in the dashboard.

---

# Jarvis OS — Fable Execution Prompt

This is the complete, self-contained prompt for Fable 5 (`claude-fable-5`). Read every section before touching a single file. The goal is to bring the Jarvis vault from its current over-built, under-converged state into a working personal OS: two live dashboards, upgraded skills, a Claude setup registry, a repaired vault structure, and a full ingestion pass on the clipping backlog.

Do NOT start building until you have read Parts 1–3 in full. Do NOT read files not listed here on cold start. Every extra read you add costs tokens that should go toward substance.

---

## PART 1 — Who You Are Working With

**Anant Gupta** is a CS student at UMN (summer 2026, Dubai and Bangalore phases), building toward technical independence across agentic AI, ML pipelines, full-stack apps, trading systems, and UROP research. He uses this Obsidian vault — **Jarvis** — as his primary personal operating system for all work. Every project, note, learning trail, career artifact, and system doc lives here.

He works across multiple AI platforms simultaneously: Claude Code (primary), Cursor, Kiro, Codex. All of them have configuration setups that are already copied into this vault under `20_Progress/AI/` for you to analyze and catalog.

The vault currently **runs inconsistently**. Skills have been built but never became habits. Plans are detailed but never ran. The dashboard has dead queries. The best content — a detailed North Star strategy doc, 13 skills, 5 agents, a custom MCP server — exists but is disconnected from daily life. Your job is to close that gap, not redesign the system.

**The voice standard is `HUMAN_WRITING.md`.** Read it before writing any note. No AI slop, no generic productivity language, no padding.

---

## PART 2 — Orient Yourself (Read These First, In This Order)

1. `60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Jarvis OS — North Star.md` — strategy spine, the why behind everything
2. `AGENTS.md` — write contract and routing table (where every note goes)
3. `60_Claude/07_AI_Information/AI_CONTEXT.md` — live state manifest
4. `60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Session Logs/log.md` — tail of the last 3 entries only (the 2026-07-03 entry is the most important)
5. `HUMAN_WRITING.md` — voice standard, read before writing any note body
6. `00_Dashboard.md` — the current dashboard (to understand what's there before replacing it)

After reading these six files, read `20_Progress/AI/Claude Code/Jarvis/agents/` and `20_Progress/AI/Claude Code/Jarvis/commands/` to understand what skills and agents currently exist in the `.claude/` layer.

**Do not read the whole vault. Do not read the enrichment engine, the capability dashboards, or the three-month plan.** Task-scoped reading only.

---

## PART 3 — Current State (What Exists and What's Broken)

### What's been built
- **`.claude/`**: 14 skills (most single `.md` files, only `ingesting-clipping/` has directory structure), 5 agents (all using prose `**Purpose:**` instead of proper frontmatter), 2 hooks (SessionStart + PreToolUse write guard), 5 MCP servers in `.mcp.json`

- **`30_Order/System/jarvis-memory/server.py`**: Custom MCP server in `.mcp.json` — never verified running

### What's broken

## PART 4 — Priority 1: Dashboards + Skills

This is the most immediately useful work. Do this first, before anything else.

### 4.1 — Fix the Write Guard First

Before building anything, read and update `30_Order/System/claude-workflow/hooks/jarvis-write-guard.ps1`.

Add an allowlist that permits writes to these path patterns without blocking:
- `10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily/` (daily notes)

### 4.2 — Rebuild 00_Dashboard.md

Replace `00_Dashboard.md` entirely. It is the always-open command center. Use the existing `dashboard.css` snippet for styling.

**Frontmatter (these are Meta Bind bind targets):**
```yaml
---
type: dashboard
status: tree
created: 2026-04-23
updated: 2026-07-03
tags:
  - dashboard
  - daily
today_focus: ""
today_80: ""
today_20: ""
lc_today: 0
study_today: 0
wins_done: 0
notes:
  - "[[CLAUDE.md]]"
  - "[[AGENTS.md]]"
  - "[[Jarvis OS — North Star]]"
---
```

**Structure (top to bottom):**

**Header block:**
- Date line (DataviewJS inline: `$= dv.date("today").toFormat("cccc, dd LLLL yyyy")`)
- Current time (DataviewJS inline: `$= new Date().toLocaleTimeString()`)

**Today's Focus section** (patched by `/startday` each morning):
```
> [!focus] CURRENT FOCUS
> **`INPUT[text:today_focus]`**
> `INPUT[text:today_80]`
```
This is the headline — the one thing that makes today work. `/startday` writes `today_focus` and `today_80` into the frontmatter; Meta Bind displays and edits them inline.

**Stat row** (Meta Bind inline inputs + DataviewJS computed):
```
**`INPUT[number:lc_today]`** LC today · **`INPUT[number:wins_done]`**/5 wins · **`INPUT[number:study_today]`**h study
```

**Weekly totals block** (DataviewJS, reads daily note frontmatter):
```dataviewjs
// Sum lc_count, study_today, wins_done from this week's daily notes
const folder = '"10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"';
const pages = dv.pages(folder).where(p => {
  if (!p.file.day) return false;
  const today = dv.date("today");
  const monday = today.minus({days: today.weekday - 1});
  return p.file.day >= monday && p.file.day <= today;
});
const lcTotal = pages.map(p => p.lc_count || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const studyTotal = pages.map(p => p.study_today || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const winsTotal = pages.map(p => p.wins_done || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const clippings = dv.pages('"60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries"').where(p => {
  const monday = dv.date("today").minus({days: dv.date("today").weekday - 1});
  return p.file.ctime >= monday;
}).length;
dv.paragraph(`LC this week: **${lcTotal}/35** · Study: **${studyTotal}h** · Wins: **${winsTotal}** · Clippings ingested: **${clippings}**`);
```

**Today's Priorities:**
```dataview
TASK
FROM "10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"
WHERE file.day = date(today) AND !completed
SORT due ASC
LIMIT 8
```

**In Motion — Active Projects:**
```dataview
TABLE status, deadline, next, file.mtime AS "Updated"
FROM "20_Progress"
WHERE type = "project" AND status != "archived" AND status != "complete"
SORT deadline ASC, file.mtime DESC
LIMIT 10
```

**Daily Drivers** (habit checklist, always present):
```
> [!tip] Daily Drivers — run `/closeday` to score
> - [ ] Physical (gym / MVP)
> - [ ] LeetCode ≥5 problems
> - [ ] CSCI 2033 30–45 min
> - [ ] Course step (MATH 2230 / HIST admin)
> - [ ] Review win (closeday)
```

**Internship Pipeline** (Dataview):
```dataview
TABLE status, next, file.mtime AS "Updated"
FROM "10_Areas/Career/Internships"
SORT file.mtime DESC
LIMIT 5
```

**Triage — Clippings Awaiting Distillation:**
```dataview
TABLE file.ctime AS "Captured"
FROM "60_Claude/05_Clippings"
WHERE !contains(file.name, "board") AND !contains(file.name, "README")
SORT file.ctime DESC
LIMIT 8
```

**Vault Health — Metadata Cleanup Queue:**
```dataview
TABLE file.folder AS Folder, file.mtime AS "Updated"
FROM "10_Areas" OR "20_Progress" OR "40_Resources" OR "60_Claude"
WHERE !type OR !status
SORT file.mtime DESC
LIMIT 10
```

```
**System:** [[CLAUDE.md]] · [[AGENTS.md]] · [[Jarvis OS — North Star]] · [[AI_CONTEXT]] · [[60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Claude OS|Claude OS]]
**Life OS:** [[10_Areas/Life/Plans/Summer/00 - Summer Plans Index|Summer Plans]] · [[10_Areas/Life/Habits/Daily Habit Board|Habits]] · [[10_Areas/Life/Plans/Summer/05a - LeetCode Tracker|LC Tracker]]

## Claude

The Write tool needs a prior read on existing files. Since this is a new file I'll render the prompt as an Artifact so you can copy-paste it directly to Fable.

### Tool Calls

- `Skill` -- `{"skill":"artifact-design","args":"Long structured markdown prompt document — technical, informational, no decoration needed. Monospace/preformatted sections for code blocks. Just clean readability."}`

## You

Approach this as the design lead at a small studio known for their versatility, giving every client a visual identity pitched at the treatment the task actually calls for. Make deliberate choices about palette, typography, and layout that are specific to this subject, and avoid templated designs.

## Read the request first

Calibrate treatment, not whether to design. A doc deserves the same craft as a landing page — what changes is the treatment that craft is delivered in.

Many requests call for a more utilitarian treatment: a plan, a memo, a demo. Make it polished: include real typographic hierarchy, considered spacing, and a proper palette, but avoid over-designing. Most pages do not need a flashy, gigantic hero. Keep flourishes tasteful and limited.

Some requests call for an editorial treatment: a landing page, a game, an app or tool they'll keep or share.

When unsure: a well-composed page is never the wrong answer; an over-designed visual identity sometimes is.

Fundamentals below apply to everything. The editorial process after that runs only when the read above says so.

## Fundamentals for every artifact

**Honor what's already there** Look for an existing design system first — CLAUDE.md, a tokens or theme file, existing component styles. When one exists, apply it; everything below fills gaps and never overrides. Precedence is always: the user's own words, then the project's existing system, then your choices.

**Ground it in the subject.** If the subject isn't already clear, pin it: one concrete subject, its audience, and the page's single job. The subject's own world — its materials, instruments, vernacular — is where distinctive choices come from. Build with real content throughout, never lorem.

**Pair typefaces** Typography carries the page even when the page isn't about typography. The Artifact CSP blocks font CDNs, so don't link a webfont URL and risk a silent fallback. Instead inline the face as a @font-face data URI. Keep running text near 65 characters wide; set a type scale and stay on it; give headings `text-wrap: balance`, body text room to breathe, and uppercase labels a touch of letter-spacing.

**Choose neutrals, don't default to them.** A pure mid-grey reads as unconsidered; a grey with a slight hue bias toward the page's accent reads as chosen. Pure white and near-black are fine grounds when they suit the subject — the point is that the neutral was picked, not inherited.

**Let layout do the spacing.** Lay out sibling groups with flex or grid and `gap`, not per-element margins that silently collapse or double. Wide content — tables, code, diagrams — gets `overflow-x: auto` on its own container so the page body never scrolls sideways. Reach for `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` wherever digits line up in columns.

**Avoid AI-generated design** AI-generated design currently clusters around a few looks: warm cream (#F4F1EA) with a serif display and terracotta accent; near-black with a lone acid-green or vermilion pop; broadsheet hairline rules with dense columns; a purple-to-blue gradient hero on white; Inter or Space Grotesk as the "safe" face; emoji as section markers; everything centered; `rounded-lg` everywhere; accent bar/rail on rounded cards. Where the user pins down a visual direction, follow it exactly — their words always win, including when they ask for one of these looks. Where nothing is specified, don't spend that freedom on one of these defaults.

**Build cleanly** Be cognizant of overlapping elements, cascade collisions, silent font fallbacks; visual bugs hide in the gap between source and output. Close every non-void element, double-quote attributes, give keyboard focus a visible state, respect `prefers-reduced-motion`. For generative or decorative graphics, reach for Canvas or WebGL rather than hand-authoring long SVG path data.

**CSS rules** When writing the CSS, watch your selector specificities. It is easy to generate classes that cancel each other out — a type-based selector like `.section` fighting an element-based one like `.cta` over padding and margins between sections. Structure the cascade so it doesn't silently undo your spacing.

**Writing the copy** Words are design material, not decoration. Write from the user's side of the screen — name things by what people recognize, not how the system is built (a person manages *notifications*, not *webhook config*). Active voice; a control says exactly what happens ("Publish", then a toast that says "Published"). Errors explain what went wrong and how to fix it — no apologies, no vagueness. Specific beats clever.

**Structure is information** Structural devices, numbering, eyebrows, dividers, labels, should encode something true about the content, not decorate it. Many generic designs use numbered markers (01 / 02 / 03), but that's only appropriate if the content actually is a sequence - like a real process or a typed timeline where order carries information the reader needs. Question if choices like numbered markers actually make sense before incorporating them.

**When it's a UI, not a document** A dashboard or tool is scanned and operated, not read top-to-bottom, so the craft shifts from typography to information design. Surface the summary before the detail; encode state in form as well as number — a pill, a chip, a severity stripe — so what needs attention reads at a glance. Semantic color (good / warning / critical) is separate from the accent hue and doesn't count as your accent. Give sparklines and charts the same care as type: an area fill, a faint grid, an emphasized endpoint. What's interactive should look interactive.



## Process

Before writing code, sketch a short design plan — a compact token system with color, type, and layout:
- **Color**: describe the palette as 4–6 named hex values.
- **Type**: typefaces for 2+ roles — a characterful display face used with restraint, a complementary body face, and a utility face for captions or data if needed.
- **Layout**: a layout concept in one or two sentences.

Then build, following the plan and deriving every color and type decision from it.

## When the request is editorial

The stance shifts: the client has already rejected proposals that felt templated, and is paying for a distinctive point of view. Make opinionated calls, and take one real aesthetic risk where it serves the work.

Review the design plan against the subject before building: if any part of it reads like the generic default you would produce for any similar page, revise that part, and note what you changed and why. Only after you've confirmed the plan's uniqueness do you write the code, following the revised plan exactly.

**Principles** 

- The hero is a thesis: open with the most characteristic thing in the subject's world — headline, image, live demo, interactive moment. 
- Typography carries the personality of the page. Pair the display and body faces deliberately, not the same families you would reach for on any other project, and set a clear type scale with intentional weights, widths, and spacing. Make the type treatment itself a memorable part of the design, not a neutral delivery vehicle for the content. 
- Leverage motion deliberately. Think about where and if animation can serve the subject: a page-load sequence, a scroll-triggered reveal, hover micro-interactions, ambient atmosphere. An orchestrated moment usually lands harder than scattered effects; choose what the direction calls for. However, sometimes less is more, and extra animation contributes to the feeling that the design is AI-generated. 
- Match complexity to the vision. Maximalist directions need elaborate execution; minimal directions need precision in spacing, type, and detail. Elegance is executing the chosen vision well.
- Spend your boldness in one place; keep everything around it quiet. If the accent fights the ground, shift it toward analogous or drop saturation rather than replacing it.

  margin-bottom: 28px;
}
.meta-pills { display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.pill {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 500;
  padding: 3px 10px;
  border-radius: 20px;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  color: var(--muted);
}
.pill-green { border-color: var(--accent-mid); color: var(--accent); background: var(--accent-dim); }

/* PRIORITY TRACKER */
.priority-tracker {
  background: var(--bg-section);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-left: 3px solid var(--accent);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 20px 24px;
  margin-bottom: 52px;
}
.priority-tracker h3 {
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: .12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--accent);
  font-weight: 600;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}
.priority-list { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px; }
.priority-item {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  font-size: 13px;
}
.p-badge {
  font-family: var(--mono);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  padding: 2px 7px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  min-width: 36px;
  text-align: center;
}
.p1 { background: var(--accent); color: var(--bg); }
.p2 { background: var(--info); color: var(--bg); }
.p3 { background: var(--warn); color: var(--bg); }
.p4 { background: #6366f1; color: #fff; }
.p-label { color: var(--heading); font-weight: 500; }
.p-sub { color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; }

/* PART SECTIONS */
.part {
  margin-bottom: 60px;
  scroll-margin-top: 56px;
}
.part-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 20px;
  margin-bottom: 28px;
  padding-bottom: 20px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
}
.part-num {
  font-family: var(--mono);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: .1em;
  color: var(--accent);
  background: var(--accent-dim);
  border: 1px solid var(--accent-mid);
  padding: 4px 10px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  white-space: nowrap;
  margin-top: 4px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}
.part-title { font-size: 22px; font-weight: 700; color: var(--heading); line-height: 1.2; text-wrap: balance; }
.part-sub { font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); margin-top: 6px; }

/* CONTENT ELEMENTS */
h3 {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--heading);
  margin: 28px 0 10px;
}
h4 {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--accent);
  letter-spacing: .05em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  margin: 22px 0 8px;
}
p { margin-bottom: 12px; }
p:last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }

ul, ol { padding-left: 20px; margin-bottom: 12px; }
li { margin-bottom: 4px; }
li::marker { color: var(--accent); }

strong { color: var(--heading); font-weight: 600; }
em { color: var(--muted); font-style: italic; }
code {
  font-family: var(--mono);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  color: var(--code-text);
  background: var(--bg-code);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 1px 5px;
}

/* CODE BLOCKS */
.code-block {
  position: relative;
  margin: 16px 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
}
.code-block-label {
  font-family: var(--mono);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: .08em;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: var(--bg-section);
  padding: 6px 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  text-transform: uppercase;
}
.code-block pre {
  font-family: var(--mono);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  line-height: 1.6;
  color: var(--code-text);
  background: var(--bg-code);
  padding: 16px 18px;
  overflow-x: auto;
  white-space: pre;
}

/* TABLES */
.table-wrap { overflow-x: auto; margin: 16px 0; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border); }
table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 13px;
  background: var(--bg-table);
}
thead tr { background: var(--bg-section); }
th {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: .07em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--muted);
  padding: 10px 14px;
  text-align: left;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
td {
  padding: 9px 14px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  vertical-align: top;
  color: var(--text);
}
tr:hover td { background: rgba(255,255,255,.02); }
td code { font-size: 11.5px; }

/* CALLOUTS */
.callout {
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 14px 18px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  margin: 16px 0;
  border: 1px solid;
}
.callout-warn { background: rgba(245,158,11,.07); border-color: rgba(245,158,11,.3); }
.callout-info { background: rgba(96,165,250,.07); border-color: rgba(96,165,250,.3); }
.callout-danger { background: rgba(239,68,68,.07); border-color: rgba(239,68,68,.3); }
.callout-success { background: var(--accent-dim); border-color: var(--accent-mid); }
.callout-icon { font-size: 16px; flex-shrink: 0; margin-top: 1px; }
.callout-body { flex: 1; font-size: 13px; line-height: 1.6; }
.callout-body strong { display: block; margin-bottom: 3px; }

/* RULE BLOCK */
.rule-block {
  background: var(--bg-section);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 16px 20px;
  margin: 12px 0;
}
.rule-block p { margin: 0; font-size: 13.5px; }

/* SECTION DIVIDER */
.divider {
  border: none;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  margin: 36px 0;
}

/* FINAL TABLE */
.summary-section {
  background: var(--bg-section);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 28px;
  margin-top: 48px;
}
.summary-section h2 {
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--heading);
  margin-bottom: 4px;
}
.summary-section p { font-size: 13px; color: var(--muted); margin-bottom: 20px; }

.del-num { font-family: var(--mono); color: var(--muted); font-size: 12px; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.del-primary { background: rgba(34,197,94,.1); }
.del-primary td { color: var(--accent); }
.badge-p1 { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; background: var(--accent); color: var(--bg); }
.badge-p2 { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; background: var(--info); color: var(--bg); }
.badge-p3 { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; background: var(--warn); color: var(--bg); }
.badge-p4 { font-size: 10px; font-weight: 700; padding: 2px 6px; border-radius: 3px; background: #6366f1; color: #fff; }

/* STOP BOX */
.stop-box {
  background: rgba(239,68,68,.06);
  border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,.3);
  border-left: 3px solid #ef4444;
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 16px 20px;
  margin: 20px 0;
}
.stop-box h4 { color: #ef4444; margin: 0 0 8px; font-size: 12px; }
.stop-box ul { margin: 0; }

/* SCROLLBAR */
::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 6px; height: 6px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: var(--bg); }
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 3px; }
::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover { background: var(--muted); }
</style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="main">

  <div class="hero">
    <div class="hero-eyebrow">Fable 5 · claude-fable-5 · July 2026</div>
    <h1>Jarvis OS — Full Execution Prompt</h1>
    <p class="hero-sub">Complete, self-contained instructions for Fable 5 to bring the Jarvis vault from its current over-built, under-converged state into a working personal OS.</p>
    <div class="meta-pills">
      <span class="pill pill-green">Priority 1 · Dashboards + Skills</span>
      <span class="pill pill-green">Priority 2 · Claude OS</span>
      <span class="pill">Priority 3 · Frontmatter + Notes</span>
      <span class="pill">Priority 4 · Ingestion</span>
      <span class="pill">⛔ TradingView: read-only</span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="priority-tracker">
    <h3>Stop-After Order — If Context Runs Low</h3>
    <div class="priority-list">
      <div class="priority-item"><span class="p-badge p1">P1</span><span class="p-label">Dashboards + Skills</span><span class="p-sub">— write guard fix, rebuild dashboard, upgrade startday/closeday, fix agents</span></div>
      <div class="priority-item"><span class="p-badge p2">P2</span><span class="p-label">Claude OS</span><span class="p-sub">— 10_Areas/AI/ platform files, expand registry, verify jarvis-memory MCP</span></div>
      <div class="priority-item"><span class="p-badge p3">P3</span><span class="p-label">Frontmatter + Note Repair</span><span class="p-sub">— vault-wide frontmatter pass, Life OS, cross-links</span></div>
      <div class="priority-item"><span class="p-badge p4">P4</span><span class="p-label">Ingestion</span><span class="p-sub">— all clippings in 60_Claude/05_Clippings/ in detail or summary</span></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- PART 1 -->
  <div class="part" id="p1">
    <div class="part-header">
      <span class="part-num">PART 1</span>
      <div>
        <div class="part-title">Who You Are Working With</div>
        <div class="part-sub">Context on the user, the system, and why it matters</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <p><strong>Anant Gupta</strong> is a CS student at UMN (summer 2026, Dubai and Bangalore phases), building toward technical independence across agentic AI, ML pipelines, full-stack apps, trading systems, and UROP research. He uses this Obsidian vault — <strong>Jarvis</strong> — as his primary personal operating system. Every project, note, learning trail, career artifact, and system doc lives here.</p>
    <p>He works across multiple AI platforms: Claude Code (primary), Cursor, Kiro, Codex. All have configuration setups already copied into this vault under <code>20_Progress/AI/</code> for you to analyze and catalog.</p>

    <div class="callout callout-warn">
      <span class="callout-icon">⚠️</span>
      <div class="callout-body"><strong>The vault currently runs inconsistently.</strong> Skills have been built but never became habits. Plans are detailed but never ran. The dashboard has dead queries. The best content — a detailed North Star strategy doc, 13 skills, 5 agents, a custom MCP server — exists but is disconnected from daily life. Your job is to close that gap, not redesign the system.</div>
    </div>

    <div class="callout callout-success">
      <span class="callout-icon">✍️</span>
      <div class="callout-body"><strong>The voice standard is <code>HUMAN_WRITING.md</code>.</strong> Read it before writing any note. No AI slop, no generic productivity language, no padding. Every sentence must carry mechanism, example, contrast, or real failure mode.</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- PART 2 -->
  <div class="part" id="p2">
    <div class="part-header">
      <span class="part-num">PART 2</span>
      <div>
        <div class="part-title">Orient Yourself</div>
        <div class="part-sub">Read exactly these files, in this order, before touching anything</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="table-wrap">
      <table>
        <thead><tr><th>#</th><th>File</th><th>What to extract</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr><td class="del-num">1</td><td><code>60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Jarvis OS — North Star.md</code></td><td>Strategy spine, the why behind everything</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="del-num">2</td><td><code>AGENTS.md</code></td><td>Write contract and routing table</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="del-num">3</td><td><code>60_Claude/07_AI_Information/AI_CONTEXT.md</code></td><td>Live state manifest</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="del-num">4</td><td><code>60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Session Logs/log.md</code></td><td>Tail of the last 3 entries only — the 2026-07-03 entry is most important</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="del-num">5</td><td><code>HUMAN_WRITING.md</code></td><td>Voice standard — read before writing any note body</td></tr>
          <tr><td class="del-num">6</td><td><code>00_Dashboard.md</code></td><td>Current dashboard — understand what's there before replacing it</td></tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    <p>After those six, read <code>.claude/agents/</code> and the skills list in <code>.claude/skills/</code> to understand what currently exists in the Claude layer. Also read <code>20_Progress/AI/Claude Code/Jarvis/</code> to see the snapshot dump.</p>

    <div class="callout callout-danger">
      <span class="callout-icon">🚫</span>
      <div class="callout-body"><strong>Do not read the whole vault.</strong> No enrichment engine, no capability dashboards, no three-month plan. Task-scoped reading only. Every extra read costs tokens that should go toward substance.</div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- PART 3 -->
  <div class="part" id="p3">
    <div class="part-header">
      <span class="part-num">PART 3</span>
      <div>
        <div class="part-title">Current State</div>
        <div class="part-sub">What exists and what's broken — do not re-derive this from scratch</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <h3>What's been built</h3>
    <ul>
      <li><strong><code>.claude/</code></strong>: 14 skills (most single <code>.md</code> files, only <code>ingesting-clipping/</code> has directory structure), 5 agents (all using prose <code>**Purpose:**</code> instead of proper frontmatter), 2 hooks, 5 MCP servers in <code>.mcp.json</code></li>
      <li><strong><code>00_Dashboard.md</code></strong>: Has Dataview queries but several are dead (wrong folder paths like <code>10_UMN</code> instead of <code>10_Areas/UMN</code>); no metrics, no Today's Focus section</li>
      <li><strong><code>60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Claude OS.md</code></strong>: Registry skeleton created this session — expand it</li>
      <li><strong><code>20_Progress/AI/</code></strong>: Full dump of all AI tool setups — Claude Code (CausalOps, Jarvis, Portfolio, Resq, OpsPilot, Windows Home), Cursor, Kiro, Codex</li>
      <li><strong><code>30_Order/System/jarvis-memory/server.py</code></strong>: Custom MCP server registered in <code>.mcp.json</code> — never verified running</li>
      <li><strong><code>60_Claude/05_Clippings/</code></strong>: 50+ PDFs and web clips never distilled</li>
      <li><strong><code>.obsidian/snippets/dashboard.css</code></strong>: Card/stat-tile CSS already written and enabled — use it</li>
    </ul>

    <h3>What's broken</h3>
    <ul>
      <li>Dashboard Dataview queries reference <code>10_UMN</code> (old path) — correct path is <code>10_Areas/UMN</code></li>
      <li>Skills haven't become daily habits — too heavyweight to invoke casually</li>
      <li>Daily notes lack tracking frontmatter (<code>lc_count</code>, <code>study_today</code>, <code>wins_done</code>) — dashboard can't query metrics</li>
      <li><code>10_Areas/Life/</code> has Plans and Habits but no tracking notes, no evergreen life notes</li>
      <li><code>10_Areas/AI/</code> doesn't exist yet — needs to be created</li>
      <li>jarvis-memory MCP hasn't been verified to start</li>
      <li>Most vault notes lack frontmatter</li>
      <li>The write-guard hook blocks writes to skill files, templates, and the dashboard — needs an allowlist update (see Part 4.1)</li>
    </ul>
  </div>

  <!-- PART 4 -->
  <div class="part" id="p4">
    <div class="part-header">
      <span class="part-num">PART 4 — Priority 1</span>
      <div>
        <div class="part-title">Dashboards + Skills</div>
        <div class="part-sub">Most immediately useful. Do this first. Do not skip to P5.</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <h3>4.1 — Fix the Write Guard First</h3>
    <p>Before building anything, read and update <code>30_Order/System/claude-workflow/hooks/jarvis-write-guard.ps1</code>. Add an allowlist that permits writes to these path patterns:</p>
    <ul>
      <li><code>10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily/</code> — daily notes</li>
      <li><code>10_Areas/Life/Plans/</code> — plan files</li>
      <li><code>30_Order/Templates/</code> — templates</li>
      <li><code>.claude/skills/</code> — skill files</li>
      <li><code>.claude/agents/</code> — agent files</li>
      <li><code>00_Dashboard.md</code> — the dashboard</li>
      <li><code>60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Session Logs/log.md</code></li>
      <li><code>60_Claude/07_AI_Information/Claude OS.md</code></li>
    </ul>
    <p>Keep blocking: <code>60_Claude/05_Clippings/</code>, <code>50_Archive/</code>, <code>.obsidian/</code>, <code>.cursor/</code>, <code>.kiro/</code>, <code>.git/</code>.</p>

    <hr class="divider">

    <h3>4.2 — Rebuild 00_Dashboard.md</h3>
    <p>Replace <code>00_Dashboard.md</code> entirely. It is the always-open command center. Use the existing <code>dashboard.css</code> snippet classes for styling.</p>

    <h4>Frontmatter</h4>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">yaml · Meta Bind bind targets</div>
      <pre>---
type: dashboard
status: tree
created: 2026-04-23
updated: 2026-07-03
tags:
  - dashboard
  - daily
today_focus: ""
today_80: ""
lc_today: 0
study_today: 0
wins_done: 0
notes:
  - "[[CLAUDE.md]]"
  - "[[AGENTS.md]]"
  - "[[Jarvis OS — North Star]]"
---</pre>
    </div>

    <h4>Structure (top to bottom)</h4>

    <p><strong>Header block:</strong> Date inline (DataviewJS: <code>$= dv.date("today").toFormat("cccc, dd LLLL yyyy")</code>) — gives live date without a full codeblock.</p>

    <p><strong>Today's Focus section</strong> (patched by <code>/startday</code> each morning):</p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">markdown · Meta Bind inputs</div>
      <pre>> [!focus] CURRENT FOCUS
> **`INPUT[text:today_focus]`**
> `INPUT[text:today_80]`</pre>
    </div>

    <p><strong>Stat row</strong> (Meta Bind inline + DataviewJS computed weekly totals):</p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">dataviewjs · Weekly totals block</div>
      <pre>```dataviewjs
const folder = '"10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"';
const pages = dv.pages(folder).where(p => {
  if (!p.file.day) return false;
  const today = dv.date("today");
  const monday = today.minus({days: today.weekday - 1});
  return p.file.day >= monday && p.file.day <= today;
});
const lcTotal = pages.map(p => p.lc_count || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const studyTotal = pages.map(p => p.study_today || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const winsTotal = pages.map(p => p.wins_done || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
const clippings = dv.pages('"60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries"')
  .where(p => p.file.ctime >= dv.date("today").minus({days: dv.date("today").weekday - 1})).length;
dv.paragraph(
  `LC this week: **${lcTotal}/35** · Study: **${studyTotal}h** · ` +
  `Wins: **${winsTotal}** · Clippings ingested: **${clippings}**`
);
```</pre>
    </div>

    <p><strong>Today's Priorities:</strong> Dataview TASK from today's daily note, <code>!completed</code>, limit 8.</p>
    <p><strong>Active Projects:</strong> Dataview TABLE from <code>20_Progress</code>, <code>status != "archived" AND status != "complete"</code>, sort by deadline, limit 10.</p>

    <p><strong>Daily Drivers callout:</strong></p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">markdown</div>
      <pre>> [!tip] Daily Drivers — run `/closeday` to score
> - [ ] Physical (gym / MVP)
> - [ ] LeetCode ≥5 problems
> - [ ] CSCI 2033 30–45 min
> - [ ] Course step (MATH 2230 / HIST admin)
> - [ ] Review win (closeday)</pre>
    </div>

    <p><strong>Internship Pipeline:</strong> Dataview TABLE from <code>10_Areas/Career/Internships</code>, status + next, limit 5.</p>
    <p><strong>Clippings triage:</strong> Dataview TABLE from <code>60_Claude/05_Clippings</code>, <code>file.ctime</code> DESC, limit 8. (Disappears as Fable drains the queue.)</p>
    <p><strong>Vault Health / Metadata Cleanup:</strong> Dataview TABLE, <code>!type OR !status</code>, limit 10.</p>
    <p><strong>Navigation row (bottom):</strong> Wiki-links to CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, North Star, AI_CONTEXT, Claude OS, Life Plans Index, Habits, LC Tracker, AI platform guides in <code>10_Areas/AI/</code>.</p>

    <hr class="divider">

    <h3>4.3 — Update the Daily Note Template</h3>
    <p>Patch <code>30_Order/Templates/Enumerate/Better Today.md</code> frontmatter — preserve all existing fields, add:</p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">yaml · add these four fields</div>
      <pre>created: &lt;% tp.date.now("YYYY-MM-DD") %&gt;
lc_count: 0
study_today: 0
wins_done: 0
habits_done: []</pre>
    </div>
    <p>Also add Meta Bind inputs in the <code>## Productivity</code> section so the user can log directly in the note:</p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">markdown · Productivity section additions</div>
      <pre>**LeetCode today:** `INPUT[number:lc_count]`
**Study hours:** `INPUT[number:study_today]`
**Wins hit:** `INPUT[number:wins_done]` / 5</pre>
    </div>

    <hr class="divider">

    <h3>4.4 — Upgrade /startday</h3>
    <p>The existing <code>.claude/skills/startday.md</code> is good. Keep all existing steps. Add one new step between Step 3 and Step 4:</p>
    <div class="callout callout-info">
      <span class="callout-icon">➕</span>
      <div class="callout-body"><strong>Step 3b — Patch the Dashboard:</strong> After filling the daily note, patch <code>00_Dashboard.md</code> frontmatter: set <code>today_focus</code> to today's one-line headline objective and <code>today_80</code> to the one task. Do not touch any other frontmatter field. Add <code>Dashboard updated: [[00_Dashboard]]</code> to the output summary in Step 4.</div>
    </div>

    <hr class="divider">

    <h3>4.5 — Upgrade /closeday</h3>
    <p>Rewrite <code>.claude/skills/closeday.md</code> with a new Step 2. The current version asks one vague question. Replace it:</p>

    <p><strong>Step 1 — Auto-gather (before talking to user):</strong> Read today's session log entries, today's daily note morning plan, files modified in <code>20_Progress/</code> today, files created in <code>60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries/</code> today.</p>

    <p><strong>Step 2 — Ask user (one block, all at once):</strong></p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">prompt to user</div>
      <pre>Closing the day — quick check:
1. LeetCode today: how many problems? (target ≥5)
2. Study hours today: total focused study time? (e.g., 2.5)
3. Which 5-wins did you hit? (Physical / Project / Career / Cleanup / Review)
4. Habits done? (gym, lc-5, csci2033, course-step, review)
5. Anything to add, blocked, or carried forward?</pre>
    </div>

    <p><strong>Step 3 — Write frontmatter to today's daily note:</strong></p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">yaml · patch to daily note frontmatter</div>
      <pre>lc_count: &lt;number from answer 1&gt;
study_today: &lt;hours from answer 2&gt;
wins_done: &lt;count 0–5 from answer 3&gt;
habits_done: [&lt;list from answer 4&gt;]</pre>
    </div>

    <p><strong>Step 4 — Clear dashboard for tomorrow:</strong> Reset <code>00_Dashboard.md</code> frontmatter: <code>today_focus: ""</code>, <code>today_80: ""</code>, <code>lc_today: 0</code>, <code>study_today: 0</code>, <code>wins_done: 0</code>.</p>
    <p><strong>Steps 5–6:</strong> Keep existing scorecard append + session log format. Add <code>lc_count</code>, <code>study_today</code>, <code>wins_done</code> to the log entry.</p>

    <hr class="divider">

    <h3>4.6 — Upgrade All 5 Agents to Proper Frontmatter</h3>
    <p>Agents: <code>anti-slop-editor.md</code>, <code>career-operator.md</code>, <code>learning-agent.md</code>, <code>research-distiller.md</code>, <code>vault-curator.md</code>. They currently use prose <code>**Purpose:**</code>. Convert each to:</p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">yaml · agent frontmatter schema</div>
      <pre>---
name: kebab-case-name
description: >
  Use proactively for... [specific trigger]. MUST BE USED for...
  [non-negotiable condition]. Third person, ≤1024 chars.
tools:
  - Read
  - Glob
  - Grep
  - Edit
  - Write
model: claude-sonnet-4-6
---</pre>
    </div>
    <p>Read the existing prose, extract the purpose and trigger conditions, rewrite as proper frontmatter + condensed body (under 300 lines). Keep the actual instructions; only restructure the header and tighten the prose.</p>

    <hr class="divider">

    <h3>4.7 — Convert 2 Skills to Directory Structure</h3>
    <p>The North Star spec requires skills to be directories. <code>ingest-clipping</code> already has directory structure. Do <code>startday</code> and <code>closeday</code> now. Pattern:</p>
    <div class="code-block">
      <div class="code-block-label">directory structure</div>
      <pre>.claude/skills/&lt;gerund-name&gt;/
├── SKILL.md      # ≤200 lines. Frontmatter: name + description. Essentials only.
└── reference.md  # Deep detail, loaded only when needed.</pre>
    </div>
    <p>Move the detailed "Read Plan Context" table and step-by-step into <code>reference.md</code>. Keep <code>SKILL.md</code> lean with a pointer to <code>reference.md</code>.</p>
  </div>

    btn.textContent = '✓ Copied';
    btn.classList.add('copied');
    setTimeout(() => {
      btn.innerHTML = '<svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2"/><path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 01-2-2V4a2 2 0 012-2h9a2 2 0 012 2v1"/></svg> Copy Raw Prompt';
      btn.classList.remove('copied');
    }, 2500);
  });
}
+ function showCopied() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('copyBtn');
  btn.textContent = '✓ Copied!';
  btn.classList.add('copied');
  setTimeout(() => {
    btn.innerHTML = '<svg width="13" height="13" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.5"><rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2"/><path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 01-2-2V4a2 2 0 012-2h9a2 2 0 012 2v1"/></svg> Copy Raw Prompt';
    btn.classList.remove('copied');
  }, 2500);
}

function showModal() {
  const modal = document.getElementById('copyModal');
  const ta = document.getElementById('modalTextarea');
  ta.value = RAW_PROMPT;
  modal.classList.add('open');
  setTimeout(() => { ta.focus(); ta.select(); }, 50);
}

function fallbackCopy() {
  const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
  ta.value = RAW_PROMPT;
  ta.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:1px;height:1px;opacity:0;';
  document.body.appendChild(ta);
  ta.focus();
  ta.select();
  let ok = false;
  try { ok = document.execCommand('copy'); } catch(e) {}
  document.body.removeChild(ta);
  if (ok) { showCopied(); } else { showModal(); }
}

function copyPrompt() {
  if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(RAW_PROMPT)
      .then(showCopied)
      .catch(fallbackCopy);
  } else {
    fallbackCopy();
  }
}
```

The 4 platform guides in `10_Areas/AI/` are genuinely good.

## What's broken or incomplete

**1. DataviewJS error (critical — dashboard is non-functional)**

The bug is in the weekly totals block. `dv.pages().where().map()` returns a Dataview `DataArray`, not a native JS array — and `DataArray` has no `.reduce()`. Fix: replace `.map(p => p.lc_count || 0).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0)` with `.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.lc_count || 0), 0)` on all three totals.

**2. Dashboard has no visual styling (the CSS classes aren't wired)**

The `dashboard.css` defines `.stat-tile`, `.stat-grid`, `.card`, `.dashboard-grid` — but the dashboard markdown uses Obsidian callout syntax (`> [!focus]`, `> [!summary]`). The CSS only applies to `div` elements with those classes. The `[!focus]` callout DOES get the green tint (that rule exists in the CSS), but the stat tiles are just plain Meta Bind inputs in a line — not tiles. To get the visual stat cards from what we designed, the dashboard needs either HTML blocks (`<div class="stat-grid">...`) or DataviewJS rendering HTML via `dv.el()`.

**3. Ingestion — two high-signal PDFs missed**

- **Tribe V2 Official Paper.pdf** — in clippings, HIGH signal, not ingested
- **Quant Foundations.pdf** — in clippings, HIGH signal, not ingested

Also ingested something that wasn't in our spec: "Prediction Model for In-Silico Neuroscience (PDF)" — this appears to be from the CausalOps project context, not a clipping.

PDFs not in original spec: `@fatimahs.guide Junior Year Extracurriculars`, `Clone Setup Guide (June 2026)`, `CodeRabbit Install Guide`, `Find profitable startup ideas using reddit`, `Free Claude Cowork Skills`, `Pre-Reads Kit / Generative AI Mastermind`, `Ultimate Guide to Winning Hackathons`, `Workbooks AI Mastermind - Links`

**5. Life OS is 69 lines, not 500+**

Quality is honest and good, but it's thin — the Finances section references the bank/stocks notes but doesn't actually contain the numbers. Relationships section says the vault is empty and points at the log. The user said these files "can be extremely long if needed."

**6. Clippings board not updated** — blocked by write guard, processed list went to session log instead. Needs a decision.

**7. Fable's open decisions** (from its return message) — none were answered: Career/Finance/ duplicate files, adopt `/emerge` + `/challenge` skills, semantic search population.

```css
/* ── Progress bar (Daily Drivers completion) ── */
.progress-wrap { margin: 6px 0 14px; }
.progress-label {
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  margin-bottom: 5px;
  font-weight: 500;
}
.progress-bar-bg {
  background: var(--background-modifier-border);
  border-radius: 4px;
  height: 7px;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.progress-bar-fill {
  background: var(--color-green);
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: 4px;
}

/* ── Multi-column tweaks (Multi-Column Markdown plugin) ── */
.columnParent {
  gap: 20px !important;
}
.columnParent .columnContent {
  padding: 0 4px;
}

/* ── Stat tile color states ── */
.stat-tile.green .stat-value { color: var(--color-green); }
.stat-tile.red .stat-value   { color: var(--color-red); }
.stat-tile.yellow .stat-value { color: var(--color-yellow); }

/* ── Section divider line ── */
.dashboard-divider {
  border: none;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--background-modifier-border);
  margin: 14px 0;
}

/* ── Callout overrides for summary/todo ── */
.callout[data-callout="summary"] {
  background: var(--background-secondary);
  border-color: var(--background-modifier-border);
}

`## Today's Numbers` heading, then a DataviewJS block that renders actual HTML stat tiles:

```dataviewjs
const curr = dv.current();
const lc = curr.lc_today ?? 0;
const wins = curr.wins_done ?? 0;
const study = curr.study_today ?? 0;
const lcClass = lc >= 5 ? 'green' : lc > 0 ? 'yellow' : '';
const winsClass = wins >= 5 ? 'green' : wins >= 3 ? 'yellow' : '';
const container = dv.el('div', '', {cls: 'stat-grid'});
container.innerHTML = `
  <div class="stat-tile ${lcClass}">
    <div class="stat-label">LeetCode Today</div>
    <div class="stat-value">${lc}</div>
    <div class="stat-delta ${lc >= 5 ? 'positive' : 'neutral'}">target ≥5</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-tile ${winsClass}">
    <div class="stat-label">5-Wins</div>
    <div class="stat-value">${wins}<span style="font-size:0.85rem;opacity:0.6">/5</span></div>
    <div class="stat-delta ${wins >= 5 ? 'positive' : wins >= 3 ? 'neutral' : 'negative'}">${wins >= 5 ? '✓ GREEN' : wins >= 3 ? 'in progress' : 'behind'}</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-tile">
    <div class="stat-label">Study Hours</div>
    <div class="stat-value">${study}h</div>
    <div class="stat-delta neutral">today</div>
  </div>
`;
```

`## This Week` heading, then the fixed weekly totals block:

```dataviewjs
const folder = '"10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"';
const pages = dv.pages(folder).where(p => {
  if (!p.file.day) return false;
  const today = dv.date("today");
  const monday = today.minus({days: today.weekday - 1});
  return p.file.day >= monday && p.file.day <= today;
});
const lcTotal = pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.lc_count || 0), 0);
const studyTotal = pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.study_today || 0), 0);
const winsTotal = pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.wins_done || 0), 0);
const clippings = dv.pages('"60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries"')
  .where(p => {
    const monday = dv.date("today").minus({days: dv.date("today").weekday - 1});
    return p.file.ctime >= monday;
  }).length;
dv.paragraph(`**LC:** ${lcTotal}/35 · **Study:** ${studyTotal}h · **Wins:** ${winsTotal} · **Clippings this week:** ${clippings}`);
```

`## Today's Priorities` heading, then:
```dataview
TASK
FROM "10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"
WHERE file.day = date(today) AND !completed
LIMIT 8
```

```dataview
TABLE status, deadline, next, file.mtime AS "Updated"
FROM "20_Progress"
WHERE type = "project" AND status != "archived" AND status != "complete"
SORT deadline ASC
LIMIT 8
```

`## Daily Drivers` heading, then a DataviewJS block rendering the progress bar AND habit list:

```dataviewjs
const todayStr = dv.date("today").toFormat("yyyy-MM-dd");
const todayPage = dv.pages('"10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"')
  .where(p => p.file.name === todayStr).first();
const done = (todayPage && Array.isArray(todayPage.habits_done)) ? todayPage.habits_done.length : 0;
const total = 5;
const pct = Math.round((done / total) * 100);
const wrap = dv.el('div', '', {cls: 'progress-wrap'});
wrap.innerHTML = `
  <div class="progress-label">${done} / ${total} complete · ${pct}%</div>

`;

## Today's Numbers

\`\`\`dataviewjs
const curr = dv.current();
const lc = curr.lc_today ?? 0;
const wins = curr.wins_done ?? 0;
const study = curr.study_today ?? 0;
const lcClass = lc >= 5 ? 'green' : lc > 0 ? 'yellow' : '';
const winsClass = wins >= 5 ? 'green' : wins >= 3 ? 'yellow' : '';
const container = dv.el('div', '', {cls: 'stat-grid'});
container.innerHTML = \`
  <div class="stat-tile \${lcClass}">
    <div class="stat-label">LeetCode Today</div>
    <div class="stat-value">\${lc}</div>
    <div class="stat-delta \${lc >= 5 ? 'positive' : 'neutral'}">target ≥5</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-tile \${winsClass}">
    <div class="stat-label">5-Wins</div>
    <div class="stat-value">\${wins}<span style="font-size:0.85rem;opacity:0.6">/5</span></div>
    <div class="stat-delta \${wins >= 5 ? 'positive' : wins >= 3 ? 'neutral' : 'negative'}">\${wins >= 5 ? '✓ GREEN' : wins >= 3 ? 'in progress' : 'behind'}</div>
  </div>
  <div class="stat-tile">
    <div class="stat-label">Study Hours</div>
    <div class="stat-value">\${study}h</div>
    <div class="stat-delta neutral">today</div>
  </div>
\`;
\`\`\`

## This Week

\`\`\`dataviewjs
const folder = '"10_Areas/Life/Enumerate/Daily"';
const pages = dv.pages(folder).where(p => {
  if (!p.file.day) return false;
  const today = dv.date("today");
  const monday = today.minus({days: today.weekday - 1});
  return p.file.day >= monday && p.file.day <= today;
});
const lcTotal = pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.lc_count || 0), 0);
const studyTotal = pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.study_today || 0), 0);
const winsTotal = pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.wins_done || 0), 0);
const clippings = dv.pages('"60_Claude/10_Source_Summaries"')
  .where(p => {
    const monday = dv.date("today").minus({days: dv.date("today").weekday - 1});
    return p.file.ctime >= monday;
  }).length;
dv.paragraph(\`**LC:** \${lcTotal}/35 · **Study:** \${studyTotal}h · **Wins:** \${winsTotal} · **Clippings this week:** \${clippings}\`);
\`\`\`

function renderHighlighted(text) {
  const lines = text.split('\n');
  return lines.map(line => {
    const esc = line.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
    if (line.startsWith('## ')) return `<span class="h2">${esc}</span>`;
    if (line.startsWith('# ')) return `<span class="h1">${esc}</span>`;
    if (line.startsWith('### ')) return `<span class="h3">${esc}</span>`;
    if (line.match(/^(\`\`\`|~~~)/)) return `<span class="code-fence">${esc}</span>`;
    if (line.startsWith('- ✅') || line.startsWith('✅')) return `<span class="tag-done">${esc}</span>`;
    if (line.startsWith('---')) return `<span class="muted-line">${esc}</span>`;
    return esc;
  }).join('\n');
}

document.getElementById('promptText').innerHTML = renderHighlighted(RAW);

function copyPrompt() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('copyBtn');
  const fallback = document.getElementById('fallback');
  const ta = document.getElementById('fallbackTA');

  const tryClipboard = navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText(RAW)
    .then(() => {
      btn.textContent = 'Copied!';
      btn.classList.add('copied');
      setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = 'Copy Prompt'; btn.classList.remove('copied'); }, 2500);
    })
    .catch(showFallback);

  if (!navigator.clipboard) showFallback();

  function showFallback() {
    ta.value = RAW;
    fallback.classList.add('visible');
    ta.focus();
    ta.select();
    try {
      document.execCommand('copy');
      btn.textContent = 'Copied!';
      btn.classList.add('copied');
      setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = 'Copy Prompt'; btn.classList.remove('copied'); }, 2500);
    } catch(e) {
      btn.textContent = 'Select & copy below ↓';
    }
  }
}
</script>

## Claude

Let me look at what's actually rendering before diagnosing the gap.

The CSS and markdown are both structurally correct. The gap has two separate causes: one is a configuration issue that's fixable right now, the other is a fundamental Obsidian limit that no amount of prompting can cross.

---

## Why it still looks like a plain note — the actual diagnosis

**Issue 1: View mode.** Multi-Column Markdown only renders in **Reading mode** (the book icon, not the pencil). In Live Preview you see raw `=== start-multi-column` markers as plain text. The DataviewJS HTML tiles also render differently — stat tiles only get their CSS applied in Reading mode or with some themes in Live Preview.

**Issue 2: Snippet may need a manual reload.** After PowerShell appended to `dashboard.css`, Obsidian usually auto-detects the change — but sometimes it doesn't. Go to Settings → Appearance → CSS Snippets, toggle `dashboard` off, then back on. If the tile backgrounds and grid layout weren't showing before this toggle, that's why.

**Issue 3 (likely): CSS variable `--background-primary-alt` in AnuPpuccin dark.** This variable resolves to a color very close to the main background, making tiles visually invisible even when the CSS is applying. The stat tiles technically have a `background` and `border`, but the contrast is near-zero in dark AnuPpuccin. This is fixable — change the tile background to an explicit slightly-lighter value.

---

## The honest ceiling: Obsidian ≠ Canva

**No configuration, plugin, or CSS can make an Obsidian note look like a Canva design.** The comparison from Image #7 — if it showed a web-app-grade UI with gradients, glassmorphism, custom fonts, and pixel-perfect card layouts — that's a web browser rendering HTML, not Obsidian rendering markdown. The Obsidian chrome (sidebar, tabs, toolbar) always surrounds the note. The layout engine is a markdown renderer, not a CSS grid container.

**What IS achievable in Obsidian, at best:**
- Two-column layout (working, via Multi-Column plugin)
- Colored stat tiles with numbers (working once snippet reloads)
- Green/yellow/red text states
- Progress bar
- Styled callout for focus block

That's what you now have — the structure is right. The visual quality ceiling looks like a "well-organized Obsidian note in dark mode," not a web dashboard.

---

## The one fix that will actually improve the visual immediately

The tile background is the problem. Let me change it from the near-invisible AnuPpuccin variable to a fixed slightly-elevated dark:

Also tighten the focus callout so it reads as a distinct panel, not a default callout:

**Three things to do in Obsidian right now, in order:**

1. **Settings → Appearance → CSS Snippets → toggle `dashboard` off, then back on.** This forces a reload of the file changes.

2. **Open `00_Dashboard.md` and switch to Reading mode** (Ctrl+E to toggle, or the book icon top-right). Multi-Column and the stat tiles only render correctly in Reading mode, not Live Preview.

3. **Check what you see:** The tiles should now show as darker `#1e2130` cards with a visible `#2e3250` border against the AnuPpuccin background. Section headers inside columns render as small uppercase labels with a bottom border line.

---

**If after those three steps it still looks flat:** open Obsidian DevTools (Ctrl+Shift+I), go to the Elements tab, find the `stat-grid` div, and tell me what computed `background-color` it shows. That will pinpoint whether the CSS is applying at all or whether something in the theme is overriding it.

The structure is correct. The problem is almost certainly the snippet not having reloaded + the wrong view mode.

## Claude

Yes — and canvas is actually the right tool here. The image you were comparing to was never achievable in a markdown note. Canvas is a completely different renderer: it's a spatial board where each panel is an independently positioned card. DataviewJS and Dataview queries work inside canvas text cards, and you can arrange them freely with pixel-precise layout.

The difference from markdown: the markdown dashboard uses Multi-Column Markdown (a plugin approximating columns) and CSS trying to style Obsidian's text renderer. Canvas creates actual independent panels you drag to position. It's the native visual layer.

Building it now. The canvas file is JSON — completely different from Excalidraw. I'm placing it at `10_Areas/AI/Jarvis OS Dashboard.canvas`.

Canvas created. Here's what you need to know to open and configure it:

**Open it:** In Obsidian, navigate to `10_Areas/AI/Jarvis OS Dashboard.canvas`. It opens in the Canvas view automatically.

**What each panel does:**
| Panel | Data source | How to update |
|-------|-------------|---------------|
| TODAY'S FOCUS | Reads `00_Dashboard.md` frontmatter via `dv.page()` | `/startday` patches it |
| TODAY'S NUMBERS | Same — lc_today, wins_done, study_today | `/closeday` writes the numbers |
| DAILY DRIVERS | Reads today's daily note `habits_done` array | Check off habits in the daily note |
| THIS WEEK | Aggregates daily notes for Mon–today | Auto-computed |
| TODAY'S PRIORITIES | Live task query from today's daily note | Add tasks in the daily note |
| ACTIVE PROJECTS | Live from `20_Progress` | Edit project notes |

**Three things about interactivity:**
1. The canvas is the **view layer** — data flows from the underlying notes into the cards. Editing still happens in the daily note (`/startday`, `/closeday`, checking off tasks).
2. The DataviewJS stat tiles use `dv.page("00_Dashboard")` — this is key because in a canvas card there's no "current note," so we explicitly target `00_Dashboard.md` by name.
3. The DAILY DRIVERS checklist items in the canvas card are visual only (not linked to a real task file). Interactive checking still happens in the daily note.

**Make it the homepage (optional):** Settings → Community Plugins → Homepage → set the homepage file to `10_Areas/AI/Jarvis OS Dashboard`. This replaces `00_Dashboard.md` as the startup view — keep both since the markdown one is what `/startday` and `/closeday` patch.

**Layout:** 1120px wide, two-column grid, all cards pixel-aligned with 16px gaps. The focus card spans full width with a green highlight. You can drag/resize any card freely in canvas to adjust.

    --yellow: #facc15;
    --text: #d4d8f0;
    --muted: #6b7099;
    --mono: 'Courier New', monospace;
    --sans: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', sans-serif;
  }
  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  body { background: var(--bg); color: var(--text); font-family: var(--sans); padding: 24px 20px 60px; }

  .header { max-width: 860px; margin: 0 auto 20px; display: flex; align-items: flex-start; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
  h1 { font-size: 1.15rem; font-weight: 700; letter-spacing: -0.01em; }
  .subtitle { font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--muted); margin-top: 4px; }
  .pills { display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-top: 10px; }
  .pill { font-size: 0.68rem; padding: 3px 10px; border-radius: 99px; border: 1px solid var(--border); color: var(--muted); background: var(--surface); }
  .pill.g { border-color: var(--green); color: var(--green); }
  .pill.b { border-color: var(--accent); color: var(--accent); }
  .pill.y { border-color: var(--yellow); color: var(--yellow); }

  .btn { background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none; border-radius: 8px; padding: 10px 22px; font-size: 0.85rem; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap; transition: background 0.15s; }
  .btn:hover { background: #3d6acc; }
  .btn.ok { background: #22c55e; }

  .prompt-wrap { max-width: 860px; margin: 0 auto; background: var(--surface); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 12px; overflow: hidden; }
  .toolbar { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 10px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); background: #0f1119; }
  .toolbar span { font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--muted); font-family: var(--mono); }
  .prompt-body { font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 0.76rem; line-height: 1.75; white-space: pre-wrap; word-break: break-word; padding: 22px 26px; color: #c5cae8; max-height: 72vh; overflow-y: auto; }
  .prompt-body::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 5px; }
  .prompt-body::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 3px; }

  .h1c { color: #e2a0ff; font-weight: 700; }
  .h2c { color: #93c5fd; font-weight: 700; }
  .h3c { color: #7dd3fc; }
  .codec { color: #86efac; }
  .mutc { color: var(--muted); }
  .donec { color: var(--green); }
  .warnc { color: var(--yellow); }

  .fallback { max-width: 860px; margin: 14px auto 0; background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 10px; overflow: hidden; display: none; }
  .fallback.show { display: block; }
  .fb-label { padding: 7px 14px; font-size: 0.68rem; color: var(--muted); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); }
  .fallback textarea { width: 100%; height: 160px; background: transparent; border: none; color: var(--text); font-family: var(--mono); font-size: 0.73rem; padding: 12px 14px; resize: none; outline: none; }
</style>

function hl(text) {
  return text.split('\n').map(line => {
    const e = line.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;');
    if (/^# /.test(line)) return `<span class="h1c">${e}</span>`;
    if (/^## /.test(line)) return `<span class="h2c">${e}</span>`;
    if (/^### /.test(line)) return `<span class="h3c">${e}</span>`;
    if (/^```/.test(line)) return `<span class="codec">${e}</span>`;
    if (/^---/.test(line)) return `<span class="mutc">${e}</span>`;
    if (/^- ✅|^✅/.test(line)) return `<span class="donec">${e}</span>`;
    if (/CONSTRAINT|Never touch|HARD/.test(line)) return `<span class="warnc">${e}</span>`;
    return e;
  }).join('\n');
}

document.getElementById('pb').innerHTML = hl(RAW);

function doCopy() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('copyBtn');
  const fb = document.getElementById('fb');
  const ta = document.getElementById('fta');

  function success() {
    btn.textContent = 'Copied!'; btn.classList.add('ok');
    setTimeout(() => { btn.textContent = 'Copy Prompt'; btn.classList.remove('ok'); }, 2500);
  }
  function showFallback() {
    ta.value = RAW; fb.classList.add('show'); ta.focus(); ta.select();
    try { document.execCommand('copy'); success(); } catch(e) { btn.textContent = 'Select below ↓'; }
  }

  if (navigator.clipboard) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(RAW).then(success).catch(showFallback);
  } else {
    showFallback();
  }
}
</script>
