## You

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:

The session continued work on Jarvis OS — an Obsidian PKM vault powered by Claude Code. The multi-part intent across this session:

**Phase A (session start):** Fix the DataviewJS `.reduce()` bug in `00_Dashboard.md`, fix write guard to allowlist the Clippings board, prepare a comprehensive Fable Pass 2 prompt covering: dashboard visual rebuild (Multi-Column Markdown + DataviewJS HTML stat tiles), Claude OS Dashboard rebuild, Life OS expansion, and completing remaining ingestion of 9 PDFs + 10 web clips.

**Phase B (after Fable Pass 2 completed):** Build an Obsidian Canvas dashboard (`10_Areas/AI/Jarvis OS Dashboard.canvas`) as the visual layer since plain markdown couldn't match the target reference image. Iteratively redesign the canvas to 1600px width with interactive Meta Bind inputs, no-scroll layout, and precise coordinate table.

**Phase C (current):** After the canvas was built and Excalidraw MCP installed, user shared screenshots showing: (1) canvas works but has dotted grid background and no visual polish, (2) Excalidraw diagram has empty boxes (no text labels), (3) the reference image (Image #9) is actually a web browser HTML app, not Obsidian at all. Fix the canvas CSS to remove the dotted background. Fix the Excalidraw empty boxes via the `/excalidraw-diagram` skill. The user then asked to execute the excalidraw skill directly.

**Core realization:** The reference Operations Dashboard image (Image #9) is a custom HTML web application running in a browser, not an Obsidian file. This is why it has solid backgrounds, full-screen app feel, and interactive buttons that Obsidian's canvas cannot replicate.

2. Key Technical Concepts:

- **Obsidian Canvas** — `.canvas` JSON files with `nodes` (type: "text", "file", "link", "group") and `edges` arrays. Text nodes render full markdown including DataviewJS. Not truly "static" — has pan/zoom chrome, dotted grid background.
- **DataviewJS in canvas nodes** — `dv.current()` returns null in canvas context; must use `dv.page("notename")` to read another note's frontmatter explicitly
- **Meta Bind explicit file targeting** — `INPUT[text:file(00_Dashboard):today_focus]` syntax writes to a specific note's frontmatter from any canvas card or note
- **DataArray `.values.reduce()`** — Dataview's `.pages().where().map()` returns a DataArray (no native `.reduce()`); fix is `.values.reduce()` to access the underlying JS array
- **Multi-Column Markdown plugin** — renders `=== start-multi-column: ID` markers only in Reading mode, not Live Preview
- **dashboard.css CSS snippet** — defines `.stat-grid`, `.stat-tile`, `.stat-label`, `.stat-value`, `.stat-delta`, `.progress-wrap`, `.progress-bar-bg`, `.progress-bar-fill`, `.card`, `.focus-headline`; applied via Obsidian Settings → Appearance → CSS Snippets
- **Write guard (jarvis-write-guard.ps1)** — PowerShell PreToolUse hook with allowlist-first logic; `.obsidian/` blocked (Write tool cannot edit it); changes to CSS must use PowerShell
- **Excalidraw MCP** — `mcp-excalidraw-server` (yctimlin/mcp_excalidraw) installed at `30_Order/System/excalidraw-mcp/`; 26 tools; canvas server at `127.0.0.1:3000`; exports `.excalidraw` JSON compatible with Obsidian Excalidraw plugin
- **ExcaliBrain** — auto-visualizes vault wikilinks; requires `![[Name.excalidraw]]` embed in a project note to make a diagram visible
- **Canvas JSON edge format** — `{"id":"e1","fromNode":"node_id","fromSide":"right","toNode":"other_id","toSide":"left"}`
- **AnuPpuccin dark theme** — `--background-primary-alt` is near-invisible against main background; tile backgrounds need explicit hex colors (`#1e2130`)

3. Files and Code Sections:

- **`00_Dashboard.md`** (vault root)
  - Data layer for the canvas dashboard. Has 6 frontmatter fields: `today_focus`, `today_80`, `today_20`, `lc_today`, `study_today`, `wins_done`. `cssclasses: [dashboard]` added.
  - DataviewJS weekly totals block fixed: `.map().reduce()` → `.values.reduce()`
  - Full body uses Multi-Column Markdown, DataviewJS stat tiles, Meta Bind callout, Dataview queries

- **`10_Areas/AI/Jarvis OS Dashboard.canvas`** (created this session)
  - Two-column canvas layout, rebuilt twice. Final version: 1600px wide, left col x=0 w=1000, right col x=1024 w=576, gap=24px
  - 10 nodes with exact coordinates verified (left ends y=1476, right ends y=1476, nav y=1500)
  - Focus node (full width, color "4"/green): Meta Bind inputs `INPUT[text:file(00_Dashboard):today_focus]`, etc.
  - Numbers node: Meta Bind number inputs + DataviewJS stat-grid tiles using `dv.page("00_Dashboard")`
  - Drivers node (color "3"/yellow): DataviewJS progress bar reading `habits_done` from today's daily note
  - Tasks node: Dataview TASK query (renders interactive checkboxes in canvas)
  - Auto-reformatted by Obsidian after initial write (node order changed, whitespace normalized)

- **`.obsidian/snippets/dashboard.css`** (updated via PowerShell only — write guard blocks Write tool)
  - Original classes: `.dashboard-grid`, `.card`, `.card-accent`, `.card-warning`, `.section-label`, `.focus-headline`, `.stat-grid`, `.stat-tile`, `.stat-label`, `.stat-value`, `.stat-delta`, `.driver-row`, callout override for `[data-callout="focus"]`
  - Pass 2 additions: `.progress-wrap`, `.progress-label`, `.progress-bar-bg`, `.progress-bar-fill`, `.columnParent` tweaks, stat tile color states `.stat-tile.green/red/yellow`
  - This session additions:
    ```css
    .canvas-background { background-color: #0f1117 !important; background-image: none !important; }
    .canvas-wrapper { background: #0f1117 !important; }
    .canvas-controls { display: none !important; }
    ```

4. Errors and Fixes:

- **DataviewJS `.reduce()` TypeError**: `pages.map(p => p.X || 0).reduce()` fails because `.map()` on a DataArray returns another DataArray (no native `.reduce()`). Fixed by changing to `pages.values.reduce((a,p) => a + (p.X || 0), 0)` — `.values` accesses the underlying JS array.

- **Stat tiles invisible in AnuPpuccin dark**: CSS used `var(--background-primary-alt)` which resolves to nearly the same color as the main background. Fixed by using explicit `#1e2130` background and `#2e3250` border.

- **Write tool blocked for `.obsidian/` files**: The write guard denies all writes inside `.obsidian/`. Fix: all `dashboard.css` changes done via PowerShell `Get-Content → modify → Set-Content` pipeline.

- **CSS snippet not auto-applying after PowerShell write**: After PowerShell appends to dashboard.css, Obsidian needs manual toggle (Settings → Appearance → CSS Snippets → toggle off → on) to reload.

- **Multi-Column Markdown not rendering**: Only works in Reading mode. In Live Preview, raw markers show as plain text. Instruction added: press Ctrl+E to switch to Reading mode.

- **Excalidraw boxes empty (no text labels)**: The `/excalidraw-diagram` skill invocation created colored shapes but left all text content empty. Needs to be re-run with the update workflow: `import_scene` → `update_element` for each box to add text → re-export.

- **Excalidraw MCP tools not available in this session**: `ToolSearch` for excalidraw-specific tools (`create_element`, `batch_create_elements`, etc.) returned no results. The MCP server IS configured in `.mcp.json` and the canvas server IS healthy (23 elements), but the MCP stdio tools did not register in this Claude Code session's context. This is the blocking issue for fixing the empty Excalidraw boxes.

- **`dv.current()` returns null in canvas**: Canvas text nodes have no associated file, so `dv.current()` fails. Fixed by using `dv.page("00_Dashboard")` explicitly to read frontmatter from the dashboard note.

- DataviewJS reduce bug fixed in 00_Dashboard.md
- Write guard allowlisted for Clippings board

- Identified root cause of reference image mismatch: Image #9 is a web browser HTML app, not Obsidian

**Ongoing:**
- **Excalidraw empty boxes**: MCP tools not accessible in current session. The canvas server is running (127.0.0.1:3000 healthy, 23 elements). The MCP stdio server is configured in `.mcp.json` but didn't register tools in this session. Fix requires either: (a) new Claude Code session where excalidraw MCP connects successfully, or (b) manually editing the `.excalidraw` JSON file to add text to elements.

- **"Looks like Canva" gap**: Definitively resolved — Image #9 is a web app, not achievable in Obsidian's native renderer. The canvas with CSS background removal is the best native Obsidian approach. Full web-app quality would require an HTML file served via Local REST API + Surfing plugin, which is a separate future effort.

- **Canvas background CSS**: Added but needs snippet reload in Obsidian (toggle off/on in Settings).

Valid JSON with 31 elements (23 original + 8 new text elements). 

**What changed:** The Obsidian Excalidraw plugin ignores the `label: { text: "..." }` property on rectangles — that's why all boxes were blank. The correct format is a separate `text` element with `containerId` pointing to the rectangle (the same approach already used by the column headers). I added 8 companion text elements, one per content box, all bound to their respective rectangles with `textAlign: "center"` and `verticalAlign: "middle"`. Also removed the now-redundant `label` properties and set the background to dark (`#1a1b26`).

Open `10_Areas/Excalidraw/Claude OS Map.excalidraw` in Obsidian — all boxes should now show their labels. If Obsidian has it cached, close and reopen the file.
